## Expanded paired pressure accuracy batch

Preregistered and verified 137 source/input bindings for the original retained
21-checkpoint pressure material. Preserved the earlier C106/C107 worker unchanged
and added a versioned adapter for that material. Serial CUDA execution completed
all 21 models, including C37/C44/C53, on all 128 requests each. No architecture,
parameters, native proposal policy or source numerical implementation changed.
All 137 bindings still matched after the batch; observed neural-module calls
were CUDA-only. Diagnostic elapsed time is not an inference-latency measurement.

The combined comparison now has 23 checkpoints and 2,944 pressure requests. Every
request's input/reference hashes match across models, and the captured portable
equation-operator function definitions have identical ASTs. Output archives are
retained and rehashed. Results preserve absolute mean-free pressure error,
relative pressure error and equation residual, with separate density and grid
summaries. [Comparison](pressure-GPU-accuracy.md).

C53 has lower worst pressure error than C107 (0.00602154 versus 0.00815329),
while C107 is better on 122 of 128 individual pressure comparisons and 125 of
128 equation-residual comparisons. The maxima occur on different requests;
C107's worst pressure case has counts 5 by 6 by 15, density 8 cells/metre and
native frame 63. This is a useful distinction between broad improvement and
worst-case behavior, not a new architecture selection or training decision.

Across this slice the fresh work now comprises 1,558 full-state outputs plus
2,944 pressure requests, with 3,602 explicit prediction transactions. There were
no new fits. A post-batch preflight still found one unrelated compute process
and 4,969 MiB GPU usage; no uncontended latency is claimed. All lab accuracy
workers and the SQL fixture are terminal. Native project creation and the
remaining all-model coverage/reproduction work are still pending.
